### [1] [LoMC: Localized Multidirectional Correction for Refusal Suppression in Routed Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13709)
*Yan Hong,Kedong Xiu,Wei Li,Jun Lan,Huijia Zhu,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出LoMC（局部多方向校正）方法，通过支持-门控的干预框架，在不扩大干预范围的前提下提升校正容量，有效抑制路由MoE和混合MoE基础模型中的拒绝行为，同时保持通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，基于方向的宽泛编辑会扰动通用计算，而仅限专家支持区域的编辑缺乏足够容量来纠正异构的拒绝表示，因此需要一种既能保持紧凑干预足迹又能提升校正容量的方法。

Method: LoMC采用支持-然后-校正的执行顺序：先识别紧凑的编辑支持区域，然后将原型校正方向聚合成层级的校正方向，最后仅在选定的支持区域内应用秩一层级校正。通过将编辑支持作为结构门控约束，在不扩大干预范围的前提下增加校正容量。

Result: 在文本和多模态安全基准测试中，LoMC在四个路由骨干网络上显著提升了非拒绝目标响应行为，同时在紧凑干预足迹下保持了通用能力。

Conclusion: LoMC通过支持门控约束有效解决了异构拒绝表示的校正容量问题，在保持模型通用能力的同时实现了高效的受控后训练拒绝抑制。

Abstract: We study controlled post-training refusal suppression in routed MoE and hybrid-MoE foundation models, aiming to increase non-refusal target-response behavior while preserving general capability under a compact intervention footprint. Existing broad direction-based edits can perturb general-purpose computation, whereas support-only expert edits often lack sufficient capacity to correct heterogeneous refusal representations. To address this limitation, we introduce Localized Multidirectional Correction (LoMC), a support-gated intervention framework that follows a support-then-correction execution order: it first identifies a compact edit support, then aggregates prototype correction directions into layer-wise correction directions, and finally applies rank-one layer-wise correction only within the selected support. By using the edit support as a structural gating constraint, LoMC increases correction capacity without expanding the intervention scope. Experiments on text-only and multimodal safety benchmarks across four routed backbones show that LoMC substantially improves non-refusal target-response behavior while maintaining general capability under a compact intervention footprint.

</details>


### [2] [Recursively Trained Diffusion Models: Limiting Collapse Distribution and Spectral Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13796)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成模型递归训练的收敛问题，证明其会几何收敛到唯一极限分布（数据分布的高斯平滑无穷混合），揭示递归训练作为低通滤波器的作用，并提出退火截断策略可消除递归复合效应，理论在理想和带误差情形下均成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论仅限定了有限轮数的误差累积，但递归训练收敛到何种分布以及收敛速度仍属开放问题。

Method: 通过Hermite谱分解分析极限分布，识别早期停止（而非不完全学习）为关键漂移动因；提出退火截断调度并证明其收敛性；在Wasserstein-2度量下分析离散化和分数估计误差的鲁棒性。

Result: 递归训练几何收敛到唯一极限分布（无穷个高斯平滑版本数据的混合）；高阶模态（精细非高斯结构）被低通滤波强烈衰减；退火截断渐进消除递归复合；误差下分布保持在Wasserstein-2球内。

Conclusion: 递归训练导致的模型崩溃可通过适当调度截断时间得到理论上的消除，且该结论对实际误差具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recursive training of generative models on their own outputs can lead to model collapse, a compounding drift away from the true data distribution. Existing theoretical works bound finite-round error accumulation in the context of diffusion models, but two questions remain open:~what distribution does the recursion converge to, and how fast? We answer both, isolating a mechanism distinct from imperfect learning: even with perfect score estimation and exact sampling, the early stopping of the reverse diffusion (required for numerical stability) drives a progressive drift away from the data distribution. We prove that this recursion converges geometrically to a unique limiting distribution, which admits a closed-form characterization as an infinite mixture of increasingly Gaussian-smoothed versions of the data distribution. A Hermite spectral decomposition of this limit reveals that recursive training acts as a low-pass filter: higher-order modes, which encode fine non-Gaussian structure, are attenuated much more strongly than coarse modes. This spectral picture motivates annealed truncation schedules that progressively shrink truncation times across retraining rounds; we prove that any schedule converging to $0$ asymptotically eliminates recursive compounding. Finally, we show our idealized characterization is robust: in the presence of discretization and score estimation errors, the learned distribution remains in a Wasserstein-2 ball around the ideal limit, with mode-dependent contraction rates that contract high-order errors faster than low-order ones. We validate the theory on synthetic Gaussian mixtures and CIFAR-10.

</details>


### [3] [Adaptive Nucleus Truncation for Long-Form Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13982)
*Ousmane Amadou Dia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应核截断采样（ANTS），这是一种用于长序列语言模型推理的自适应解码方法。ANTS通过熵条件控制器动态调整截断宽度，并在不安全时保留无截断回退机制。在33B参数（4B激活）的稀疏MoE推理模型上，ANTS在8K、16K和32K生成预算下分别平均提升+1.9、+3.8和+5.2个百分点，在指令遵循和数学推理任务上提升尤为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有截断采样方法（如top-p、min-p、固定top-nσ）使用固定阈值，无法适应熵值变化、任务难度、训练阶段或生成预算的动态调整，限制了长序列推理的性能和稳定性。

Method: ANTS将top-nσ采样从固定解码规则扩展为自适应 rollout 控制机制：选择温度缩放前最大logit周围的标准化邻域，使用熵条件控制器动态调整截断宽度，并保留无截断回退臂以在截断不安全时稳定训练。

Result: 在33B/4B稀疏MoE推理模型上，ANTS在8K、16K、32K生成预算下平均提升+1.9、+3.8、+5.2个百分点。IFBench在32K下提升超10分，AIME 2025提升7分。Codeforces在8K时落后基线，但在16K和32K时逆转差距并显著提升ELO。

Conclusion: 采样器设计不应仅被视为解码超参数，而应作为稳定和扩展长预算推理的一部分。

Abstract: Sampling plays an important role in long-form language-model reasoning. Over thousands of decoding steps, small changes in the candidate token set can compound into different reasoning trajectories, stability profiles, and final answers. Existing truncation methods such as top-$p$, min-$p$, and fixed top-$nσ$ sampling improve over unrestricted sampling, but they rely on fixed thresholds that cannot adapt to changes in entropy, task difficulty, training stage, or generation budget. We introduce Adaptive Nucleus Truncation Sampling (ANTS), which extends top-\(nσ\) sampling from a fixed decoding rule into an adaptive rollout-control mechanism for long-form generation. ANTS selects standardized neighborhoods around the maximum logit before temperature scaling, adapts the truncation width using an entropy-conditioned controller, and retains a no-truncation fallback arm to stabilize training when truncation becomes unsafe. On a 33B-total / 4B-active sparse Mixture-of-Experts reasoning model, ANTS improves average performance over percentage-based benchmarks by +1.9, +3.8, and +5.2 points at 8K, 16K, and 32K generation budgets, respectively. The strongest gains appear on instruction following and mathematical reasoning, with IFBench improving by more than 10 points at 32K and AIME 2025 improving by 7 points. Code generation reveals an important budget interaction. On Codeforces, ANTS trails the baseline at 8K, but reverses this gap and substantially improves ELO at 16K and 32K. These results suggest that sampler design should be treated not just as a decoding hyperparameter, but as part of how we stabilize and scale long-budget reasoning.

</details>


### [4] [A General Framework for Decision Trees via Bregman Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13984)
*Mathias Bourel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Bregman散度的CART决策树泛化框架，统一了多种分裂准则并研究了相关理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CART决策树虽然应用广泛，但不同统计模型下的分裂准则通常是针对特定模型临时引入的，缺乏统一的几何和凸分析理论基础。

Method: 利用Bregman散度（包括KL散度、泊松散度、Itakura-Saito散度等）替代传统分裂准则，构建统一的决策树学习框架，并从凸生成函数的强凸性和光滑性出发分析节点不纯度增益。

Result: 得到了一个更广泛的决策树家族，能够适应不同的统计模型和底层几何结构；建立了生成函数性质与不纯度增益、估计量的稳定性和相合性之间的联系。

Conclusion: Bregman散度方法为决策树分裂准则提供了统一的凸几何理论基础，使得不同模型下的准则可以从共同原理中推导和解释。

Abstract: Decision trees are one of the fundamental tools in statistical learning due to their interpretability, flexibility, and their ability to adapt to nonlinear structures. Among them, the Classification and Regression Trees, introduced by Breiman, Friedman, Olshen, and Stone in 1984, became one of the most influential algorithms and remains one of the most widely used methods for classification and regression problems. On the other hand, Bregman divergences, introduced by Lev Bregman in 1967 in the context of convex optimization, provide a broad family of loss functions that naturally generalize the squared Euclidean distance. This family includes, among others, the Kullback-Leibler divergence, the Poisson divergence, and the Itakura-Saito divergence, as well as several losses associated with distributions belonging to the exponential family. Moreover, Bregman divergences possess a rich geometric structure and deep connections with convex analysis and information geometry. In this work, we propose a generalization of the CART paradigm based on Bregman divergences, thereby obtaining a broader family of decision trees adapted to different statistical models and underlying geometries. Although algorithms such as CART or classical implementations such as rpart incorporate different impurity criteria, these are usually introduced in an ad hoc manner for each specific model. In contrast, the Bregman divergence approach provides a unified framework that allows these criteria to be derived and interpreted from common convex and geometric principles. Beyond the algorithmic construction, we also investigate theoretical properties of these trees. In particular, we study how properties of the generating convex function -- such as strong convexity or smoothness -- influence impurity gains between parent and child nodes, as well as stability and consistency properties of the estimator.

</details>


### [5] [Geometric Domain Adaptation via Optimal Transport for Linear Regression in R^2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14023)
*Brian Britos,Mathias Bourel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了在二维空间中，当源域和目标域之间存在旋转、平移或相似变换时，使用p-范数代价（p≥2）的最优传输映射可以恢复出真实的几何变换。基于此理论，提出了结合K-means和最优传输的方法来估计变换，实现线性回归模型在目标域数据稀缺时的自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在二维空间中，当源域和目标域之间存在旋转、平移或相似变换时，最优传输能否恢复出潜在的真实映射，从而为领域自适应提供理论基础和可解释的方法。

Method: 理论证明：在ℝ²中，当源域和目标域通过旋转、平移或相似变换关联时，使用p-范数代价（p≥2）的最优传输映射可以恢复真实变换。实践方法：结合K-means聚类和最优传输来估计变换参数，实现线性回归模型的自适应。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提方法在目标域数据稀缺的情况下，性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文提供了最优传输在恢复旋转、平移和相似变换方面的理论结果，并提出了兼具概念清晰度和实际应用价值的线性回归自适应方法，强调可解释性和理论洞察而非依赖复杂的深度学习架构。

Abstract: Optimal Transport has become recently a powerful method for domain adaptation by aligning source and target distributions. We study a supervised domain adaptation problem where source and target domains are related by a rotation or a translation or a homothety in $\mathbb{R}^2$. We prove that the optimal transport map recovers the underlying map when using a $p-$norm cost with $p \geq 2$. Based on this insight, we develop a method combining $K-$means and optimal transport to estimate the underlying map, enabling adaptation of linear regression models when target data is scarce. Simulations demonstrate improved performance over baseline methods. Rather than relying on highly expressive deep learning architectures, we focus on classical machine learning models to emphasize interpretability and theoretical insight. This perspective allows us to explicitly characterize the role of optimal transport in recovering geometric transformations such as rotations, translations, and homotheties.
  Our contributions include a theoretical result linking optimal transport and rotations, translations and homothecies in $\mathbb{R}^2$, and a practical method for adaptation in linear regression offering both conceptual clarity and applied value in domain adaptation tasks in this space.

</details>


### [6] [Anytime-Valid Confirmation of Label-Shift Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14028)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件e值和非负鞅的时序验证框架，用于在目标标签稀缺时，利用预指定的标签偏移校正假设进行序贯检验，通过累积负对数预测密度（NLPD）差距将常规模型监控转化为正式统计测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小批量科学部署场景中，标注目标结果可能过于稀少，无法进行可靠的偏移估计，即使未标注的目标输入可用。作者旨在解决当从业者拥有来自领域知识的预指定标签偏移校正，并需要判断输入标签结果是否支持该校正的互补设置。

Method: 证明了每观测的似然比（标签偏移校正后的预测与源预测之间）是一个条件e值，其累积乘积构成非负鞅；利用Ville不等式得到随时有效的确认规则。对数鞅等于源预测与校正后预测之间的累积负对数预测密度（NLPD）差距。对于高斯过程源模型，推导了闭合形式解。

Result: GP回归仿真验证了该方法在第一类错误控制、有限样本功效、误校准敏感性以及小批量优势（相较于基于标签的重估计方法）方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 拒绝原假设意味着输入数据支持所提出的偏移校正（相对于源预测），但该检验并非对偏移程度的精确估计；该方法将常规模型监控转化为正式的序贯检验框架。

Abstract: In small-batch scientific deployments, labeled target outcomes may be too scarce for reliable shift estimation even when unlabeled target inputs are available. We address the complementary setting where the practitioner has a pre-specified label-shift correction from domain knowledge and asks whether incoming labeled outcomes support it. We show that the per-observation likelihood ratio between a label-shift-corrected predictive and the source predictive is a conditional e-value, so its running product is a nonnegative martingale and Ville's inequality yields an anytime-valid confirmation rule. The log martingale equals the cumulative negative log-predictive density (NLPD) gap between the source and the corrected predictive, converting routine model monitoring into a formal sequential test. Rejection means the incoming data support the posited correction relative to the source predictive, but it is not a precise estimate of the degree of shift. Closed forms are available for GP sources with Gaussian label-shift ratios. GP regression simulations validate Type I control, finite-sample power, miscalibration sensitivity, and the small-batch advantage of a reliable prior over label-based re-estimation.

</details>


### [7] [Hybrid Uncertainty Sensitivity Analysis Based on the HSIC for High-Dimensional Responses with Aleatory--Epistemic Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14053)
*Shijie Zhong,Jiangfeng Fu,Pengfei Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对混合偶然-认知不确定性下高维系统响应的全局敏感性分析难题，提出双空间张量积RKHS框架，通过双重Möbius反演实现全局依赖度的正交分解，得到纯偶然、纯认知及其交互效应的维数敏感性指标，并采用辅助变量表示和向量化单循环实现提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HSIC方法局限于单输出场景，无法对异质不确定性源及其交互作用进行严格分解，亟需一个能同时处理混合不确定性和高维输出的全局敏感性分析框架。

Method: 构建双空间张量积RKHS框架：在潜在输入空间和多维输出空间上构造因子化核函数，通过双重Möbius反演正交分解全局依赖度；引入基于逆概率积分变换的辅助变量表示处理层次不确定性和Copula相关性；设计全向量化单循环实现避免嵌套MC模拟。

Result: 在多输出Ishigami函数和气动压力场问题上的数值实验验证了所提框架的准确性、可扩展性和实际适用性。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功将HSIC-GSA推广到混合不确定性下的多输出场景，实现了不确定性源的正交分解和维数级归因，且具有统计显著性检验和计算可行性。

Abstract: Quantifying the influence of hybrid aleatory and epistemic uncertainties on high-dimensional system responses remains a major challenge in global sensitivity analysis (GSA). Existing Hilbert--Schmidt Independence Criterion (HSIC)-based approaches are primarily restricted to single-output settings and lack a rigorous decomposition of heterogeneous uncertainty sources and their interactions. To address this limitation, a novel double-space tensor-product RKHS framework is proposed for sensitivity analysis under hybrid uncertainty. By constructing factorized kernels over both the latent input space and the multidimensional output space, a concurrent double Möbius inversion is derived to orthogonally decompose the global dependence measure into pure aleatory effects, pure epistemic effects, and their interaction contributions. The resulting dimension-wise sensitivity indices preserve the uncertainty attribution structure across all output dimensions. To satisfy the independence assumptions required by the decomposition, an auxiliary-variable representation based on the inverse probability integral transform is introduced, enabling the treatment of hierarchical uncertainties and Copula-induced correlations within a unified latent space. A fully vectorized single-loop implementation is further developed to avoid the computational burden of nested Monte Carlo simulation. Statistical significance and estimation uncertainty are quantified through permutation testing and Bootstrap confidence intervals. Numerical studies on a modified multi-output Ishigami function and an aerodynamic pressure-field problem demonstrate the accuracy, scalability, and practical applicability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [8] [Gradient boosting for extremes: sampling theory and application to insurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14268)
*Stéphane Lhaut,Olivier Lopez*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了梯度提升的统计学习理论，用于估计协变量依赖的广义帕累托分布，通过对GP似然进行正交重参数化，在ERM框架下推导了非渐近误差界，并应用于医疗事故保险数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Peaks-over-Threshold建模中，需要估计协变量依赖的广义帕累托分布，但现有方法缺乏严格的统计学习理论保证，且GP似然参数间的相关性影响梯度提升的训练稳定性。

Method: 对GP似然进行正交重参数化以对角化Fisher信息矩阵，将估计问题纳入ERM框架，推导了包含统计波动、二阶规则变化下的近似偏差和有限提升迭代近似误差三部分的非渐近误差界。

Result: 重参数化显著降低了训练过程中的梯度相关性并提高了收敛稳定性；在医疗事故保险数据集上，梯度提升方法对结算成本的尾部拟合良好，发现结算天数是最主要的尾部轻重预测因子。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架明确了偏差-方差权衡，重参数化策略具有实际效益，方法在保险精算领域有良好应用前景。

Abstract: We develop a statistical learning theory for gradient boosting applied to the estimation of covariate-dependent Generalized Pareto (GP) distributions in the context of Peaks-over-Threshold modeling. After an orthogonal reparametrization of the GP likelihood that diagonalizes its Fisher information matrix, we cast the estimation problem within the Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) framework and derive non-asymptotic error bounds for the boosting estimator. Our analysis accounts for three distinct sources of error in the process: statistical fluctuations, the approximation bias inherent to the asymptotic nature of the GP model-controlled under second-order regular variation-and the approximation error associated with the finite number of boosting iterates, making explicit the resulting bias-variance trade-off. We illustrate the practical benefits of the reparametrization through simulations, showing that it significantly reduces gradient correlation during training and improves convergence stability. The methodology is applied to a medical malpractice insurance dataset from the Texas Department of Insurance, comprising over 18 000 closed claims. The gradient boosting approach yields a good fit for the tail of settlement cost distributions and reveals that the number of days to settlement is the dominant predictor of tail heaviness, consistent with earlier findings in the reserving literature.

</details>


### [9] [Nonlocal Bayesian Modeling of Continuous Spatio-Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14313)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出NonLocal Bayesian Spatio-Temporal model (NLBST)，一种分层贝叶斯框架，通过坐标基展开和连续时间ODE处理不规则时空数据中的非局部耦合，实现可追踪的贝叶斯推断与不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时空预测面临时间点不规则、空间观测稀疏、不确定性量化需求以及非局部相互作用（长程空间耦合）等挑战，现有方法难以在连续时空域中对非局部动力学进行原则性的贝叶斯推断。

Method: 1) 基于坐标的空间基展开表示潜在场；2) 系数过程用连续时间ODE建模，其可学习的线性算子对应非局部IDE的Galerkin降阶；3) 神经网络ODE残差捕获额外非线性动力学；4) 线性高斯观测模型支持缺失/不规则观测下的卡尔曼式序贯更新；5) 全局参数通过变分推断学习。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，NLBST在强非局部和部分观测场景下展现出优于基线的预测性能和空间泛化能力，且不确定性校准良好。

Conclusion: NLBST成功地将非局部IDE的物理先验与贝叶斯推断相结合，为连续的时空场建模提供了可扩展且具有理论依据的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world spatio-temporal forecasting must handle irregular time points, spatially sparse observations, and the need for uncertainty quantification. This setting is often further compounded by nonlocal interactions (long-range spatial coupling). Modeling continuous-space, continuous-time nonlocal dynamics naturally leads to infinite-dimensional integro-differential equations (IDEs), making principled Bayesian inference intractable. We propose the NonLocal Bayesian Spatio-Temporal model (NLBST), a hierarchical Bayesian framework for continuous spatio-temporal fields that learns explicit nonlocal coupling while retaining tractable inference. NLBST represents the latent field via a coordinate-based spatial basis expansion and models the coefficient process with a continuous-time ODE whose learnable linear operator corresponds to a Galerkin reduction of a nonlocal IDE; a Neural ODE residual captures additional nonlinear dynamics. A linear-Gaussian observation model enables Kalman-style sequential updates under missing and irregular observations, while the spatial basis representation enables inductive prediction at unmeasured locations without retraining. Global parameters are learned via variational inference, and uncertainty is handled through a Bayesian hierarchy. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate strong forecasting and spatial generalization with well-calibrated uncertainty, yielding substantial gains over baselines in strongly nonlocal and partially observed regimes.

</details>


### [10] [Beyond the Training Distribution: Evaluating Predictions Under Distribution Shift and Selection Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14506)
*Annie Ulichney,Amanda Coston*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双重机器学习方法，用于在协变量偏移和选择性标签同时存在的情况下，评估黑箱预测模型在目标环境中的性能风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型部署前评估其在新环境中的表现至关重要，但实际中常面临协变量偏移（目标与源分布不同）和选择性标签（结果的可观测性受历史决策影响）两大问题，现有方法未能同时解决这两者。

Method: 提出了一个双重机器学习（Double Machine Learning）程序，基于目标风险的影响函数推导出偏差校正估计量，在标准假设下证明了目标风险的可识别性。

Result: 在eICU电子健康记录数据库上的实验表明，该方法相比仅处理单一问题（选择性标签或协变量偏移）的方法以及标准插件方法，能够更准确地追踪真实目标风险。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了协变量偏移与选择性标签联合存在下的模型预部署评估问题，为算法辅助决策场景下的安全部署提供了更可靠的性能估计工具。

Abstract: Understanding how a prediction model will perform in a new environment before deployment is essential to preventing harm when algorithms inform decision-making. Two common sources of model performance degradation are (i) covariate shift, where the target covariate distribution differs from the source, and (ii) selective labels, where the observability of outcomes depends on historical decisions. We study pre-deployment model evaluation under the joint presence of covariate shift and labeling of outcomes selectively based on observed features. In particular, we present a double machine learning procedure for estimating the target risk of an arbitrary black-box prediction model under a general loss function. We show identification of this estimand under standard assumptions and derive a bias-corrected estimator based on the influence function of the target risk. Finally, we evaluate our estimator through experiments using the eICU electronic health records database, showing that it tracks the true target risk more accurately than methods that address either selective labels or covariate shift alone, as well as baselines that combine standard plug-in approaches.

</details>


### [11] [Cluster LOCO: Feature Importance For Interpreting Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14592)
*Claire M. He,Genevera I. Allen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster LOCO（Leave-One-Covariate-Out）系列方法，通过特征遮挡和聚类泛化能力来评估特征重要性，是一种与算法无关的聚类特征重要性评分框架。实验表明其在合成数据和单细胞转录组学应用中比现有方法更可靠地识别信息性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类分析在现代大规模复杂数据中应用广泛，但聚类结果难以解释、审计和复现。现有的聚类特征重要性方法往往局限于特定算法和数据假设，缺乏通用的、模型无关的特征解释方法。

Method: 提出Cluster LOCO系列方法：基于特征遮挡和聚类泛化性（即在一个子集上学习的聚类标签能否准确预测其他样本），通过移除某个特征后对泛化能力的退化程度来衡量该特征的重要性。包括Cluster LOCO-Split（基于数据分割）和Cluster LOCO-MP（基于minipatch集成，适用于大规模数据）。

Result: 在合成数据和单细胞转录组学细胞类型发现应用中，Cluster LOCO比现有聚类特征重要性方法更可靠地恢复了信息性特征。

Conclusion: Cluster LOCO提供了一种通用的、模型无关的聚类特征重要性评估框架，有效解决了聚类结果可解释性不足的问题，并在实际生物数据中展现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Clustering is widely used for exploratory analysis and scientific discovery, driving insights from market segmentation to biological data analysis, but its outputs can be difficult to interpret, audit, and reproduce as modern datasets become increasingly large and complex. Reliable use of clustering requires understanding which features drive the discovered structure, yet feature-level explanations for clustering remain scarce compared with methods in supervised learning. Furthermore, existing clustering feature importance scores are often tied to specific algorithms and data assumptions. To address these challenges, we propose Cluster LOCO (Leave-One-Covariate-Out), a family of model-agnostic feature importance scores for clustering. Cluster LOCO is built on feature occlusion and clustering generalizability, defined as whether cluster labels learned on one subset of the data can be accurately predicted on held-out samples. For any chosen clustering algorithm, Cluster LOCO quantifies a feature's importance by measuring how much its removal degrades generalizability. We first introduce Cluster LOCO-Split, which relies on data splitting, and then extend it to Cluster LOCO-MP, a minipatch ensemble-based version designed for large-scale data. Across synthetic simulations and an application to cell-type discovery in single-cell transcriptomics, we show that Cluster LOCO more reliably recovers informative features than existing clustering feature importance methods.

</details>

### [12] [Can Editing 1 Neuron Fix Repetition Loops in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13705)
*Aristotelis Lazaridis,Aman Sharma,Dylan Bates,Brian King,Vincent Lu,Jack FitzGerald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Gemma 4指令微调模型在长枚举任务中的重复崩溃问题（"doom loops"），发现该问题可定位到少量MLP神经元或路由专家，通过静态权重编辑（最小仅反转单个神经元符号）即可消除，但编辑无法解决因知识缺失导致的更高级"思考循环"问题——这本质上是知识精度缺陷而非可移除电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Gemma 4模型在长枚举任务中高发（高达95%）的重复崩溃（doom loops）现象是否可以通过权重编辑局部化并移除。

Method: 使用逐层消融和逐神经元归因定位关键神经元/专家，再通过全生成扫描确认最强候选，最后施加静态权重编辑（如符号反转）进行抑制。

Result: 重复循环可追溯到少量MLP神经元（E2B模型仅需反转单个神经元）或少量路由专家（MoE模型），编辑后循环消除且通用基准分数不受影响；但对于更长的思考预算，较大模型出现的非收敛"doom looping"（因无法回忆事实而自我纠正循环）仅被减少但未消除。

Conclusion: 权重手术可以删除循环（局部化生成病理可编辑移除），但无法补充缺失的知识；高级doom looping本质是知识精度问题，非可移除电路。

Abstract: Yes. Can it cure doom loops? Probably not.
  The Gemma 4 instruction-tuned models share a reproducible failure: on long factual enumeration prompts, such as listing every episode of a TV series, the 88 IAU constellations, or the 151 original Pokemon, they collapse into repetition, either a tight verbatim loop or a list whose entries decay onto a single answer. These loops occur at rates as high as 95% and survive prompt rewording, inference-engine changes, and most sampling adjustments. In this paper we explore whether this behavior is localized enough to remove by weight edits. To localize the cause, we use per-layer ablation and per-neuron attribution, then confirm the strongest candidates with full-generation sweeps. The loops trace to a small set of MLP neurons (or, in the 26B-A4B Mixture-of-Experts model, a few routed experts) which we suppress with static weight edits. These "surgeries" can be as small as a single sign-inverted neuron (in the E2B model). The size of the effective edits grows with model scale, but in all cases, the loop patterns can be addressed at normal generation budgets while preserving general-purpose benchmark scores. However, the edits do not solve everything: we also study longer thinking budgets, where the two larger models most visibly enter doom looping, i.e. a non-convergent regime in which the model self-corrects in circles over a fact it cannot recall, exhausting the budget without committing to a final answer. We show this residual failure is reduced but not eliminated by the same edits, and argue it is fundamentally a knowledge-precision problem rather than a removable circuit; weight surgery can delete a loop, but it cannot supply a missing fact. Our results are both a feasibility demonstration, that is, evidence that a concrete generation pathology can be localized to a few parameters and edited out, and a delineation of where that approach stops.

</details>


### [13] [Efficient On-Device Diffusion LLM Inference with Mobile NPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13740)
*Tuowei Wang,Yanfan Sun,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: llada.cpp是首个面向智能手机NPU的扩散大语言模型推理框架，通过多块推测解码、双路径渐进修订和交换优化内存运行时三项技术，在LLaDA-8B上实现相比CPU基线17-42倍延迟降低，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型(dLLM)通过并行去噪多个token加速生成，适合延迟敏感的移动端推理，但重复去噪在智能手机上带来大量计算开销。移动NPU虽然提供高吞吐密度矩阵计算，但面临token承诺导致每块有效工作负载缩小、token修订使KV缓存复用复杂、以及NPU可见地址空间有限引发昂贵的重映射和数据传输开销等挑战。

Method: 提出三项核心技术：(1) 多块推测解码(Multi-Block Speculative Decoding)——用推测的未来块token填充当前块解码后期缩小的有效工作负载；(2) 双路径渐进修订(Dual-Path Progressive Revision)——允许已承诺的token在稳定前保持可修订状态，并通过CPU侧路径刷新不稳定token，而不阻塞密集的NPU执行；(3) 交换优化内存运行时(Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime)——紧凑化NPU可见地址布局，并将数据准备与NPU计算重叠以减少重映射和传输开销。

Result: 在多种硬件平台和dLLM工作负载上评估，llada.cpp使LLaDA-8B生成延迟相比带前缀KV缓存复用的CPU基线降低17倍到42倍，且保持生成质量不变。

Conclusion: llada.cpp作为首个NPU感知的端到端推理框架，有效解决了移动NPU上加速dLLM面临的关键挑战，大幅提升移动端扩散大语言模型的推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) accelerate generation by denoising multiple tokens in parallel, making them attractive for latency-sensitive mobile inference. However, repeated denoising introduces substantial computation on smartphones. Mobile neural processing units (NPUs) offer high-throughput dense matrix computation, but efficiently exploiting them remains challenging: token commitment shrinks per-block effective workloads, token revision complicates KV cache reuse, and limited NPU-visible address space incurs costly remapping and data transfer overheads.
  In this paper, we propose llada.cpp, the first NPU-aware inference framework for accelerating dLLMs on smartphones. llada.cpp aligns block-wise dLLM inference with the execution characteristics of mobile NPUs through three techniques. (1) Multi-Block Speculative Decoding fills the shrinking workload in late-stage current-block decoding with speculative future-block tokens. (2) Dual-Path Progressive Revision keeps committed tokens revisable until stable and refreshes unstable tokens through a CPU-side path without stalling dense NPU execution. (3) Swap-Optimized Memory Runtime compacts NPU-visible address layouts and overlaps data staging with NPU computation to reduce remapping and transfer overheads. We implement llada.cpp as an end-to-end framework and evaluate it across diverse hardware platforms and dLLM workloads. llada.cpp reduces LLaDA-8B generation latency by 17x-42x over the CPU baseline with prefix KV cache reuse, while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [14] [High-Frequency Pricing at Scale for E-Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13741)
*Stefan Birr,Tobias Huelden,Mones Raslan,Adele Gouttes,Andreas Schmitt,Mateusz Koren,Johannes Stephan,Robert Streek,Manuel Kunz,Tim Januschowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向时尚电商促销活动的"预测-然后-优化"定价工具，结合梯度提升树的日级需求预测与多目标优化框架，在Zalando的23次A/B测试中实现约6%利润提升，并将定价决策时间从数小时缩短至分钟级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚电商促销活动面临需求波动剧烈、需快速定价、以及平衡短期收入与长期盈利能力的挑战，现有周粒度系统存在局限性，亟需更高时效性和更优效果的定价解决方案。

Method: 采用预测-然后-优化架构：使用梯度提升树进行日级需求预测，结合多目标优化框架同时最大化长期利润和净商品价值，覆盖超过500万件商品。

Result: 在2023-2024年期间，于Zalando的12个市场进行23次A/B测试，新系统相比先前的手动-算法混合方法实现约6%的利润提升，同时在销售额和收入上保持同等水平；定价决策时间从数小时降至分钟级。

Conclusion: 该算法已成功部署至生产环境，目前处理该公司促销活动中大部分算法定价决策，验证了预测-然后-优化架构在时尚电商促销定价中的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, development, and implementation of a specialized forecast-then-optimize algorithmic pricing tool for sales campaigns in fashion e-commerce. Sales events present unique challenges for pricing including volatile demand patterns, rapid pricing decisions, and the need to balance short-term revenue with long-term profitability. We describe our approach combining daily-resolution demand forecasting using gradient-boosted trees with a multi-objective optimization framework that maximizes both long-term profit and net merchandise value for more than 5 million articles. Our solution addresses key limitations of existing weekly-granularity systems by implementing a forecast-then-optimize architecture that reduces pricing decision time from hours to minutes. We validate our approach through 23 A/B tests across 12 markets during 2023-2024 sales campaigns at Zalando, one of Europe's leading online fashion retailers. Experimental results demonstrate that the new pricing system achieves approximately 6% higher profit while maintaining equivalent performance on sales and revenue compared to the previous manual-algorithmic hybrid approach. Based on these results, the algorithm was successfully deployed to production and now handles the majority of algorithmic pricing decisions for sales campaigns at the company.

</details>


### [15] [A fully GPU-based workflow for building physics emulators of hypersonic flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13742)
*Fabian Paischer,Dylan Rubini,Deniz A. Bezgin,Aaron B. Buhendwa,David Hauser,Florian Sestak,Johannes Brandstetter,Sebastian Kaltenbach,Nikolaus A. Adams*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU的全流程工作流，集成加速数据生成与神经网络仿真器训练，通过可微高保真求解器（JAX-Fluids）实现残差驱动的物理一致性改进，使仿真器在训练分布外仍保持可靠性，适用于高超声速流动等复杂物理场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高超声速流动中存在陡峭梯度（如激波），传统降阶模型和神经仿真器难以在工业相关应用中物理一致地捕捉流场拓扑结构，亟需一种兼具高保真度和低计算成本的替代方案。

Method: 1）构建全GPU工作流，集成加速数据生成与神经网络训练；2）采用可微高保真求解器JAX-Fluids快速生成数据集并计算残差；3）通过残差驱动（residual-based refinement）改进神经仿真器，增强物理一致性；4）分析多种模型架构的标度行为，评估其优劣。

Result: 1）揭示了不同模型架构的标度行为及其优缺点；2）残差驱动精炼使得仅需网格和输入参数即可训练，显著降低残差并提升物理一致性；3）可微仿真与残差精炼结合使仿真器在训练分布外仍保持可靠性。

Conclusion: 该工作流有效解决了高超声速流动中神经仿真器物理一致性不足的问题，为代理模型在真实工程设计循环中的部署提供了关键支撑。

Abstract: The ability to resolve complex physical phenomena with high fidelity and at low computational cost is central to addressing key challenges in modern engineering. A prime example lies in hypersonic flows, where the precise prediction of the full flowfield topology, in particular with respect to shock wave location and intensity, is critical. Yet supersonic and hypersonic flows continue to be a stumbling block for traditional reduced-order models and neural emulators that struggle to capture steep gradients in flow states with physical consistency in applications of industrial relevance. To that end, we introduce a fully GPU based workflow that integrates accelerated data generation with the training of neural emulators augmented by uncertainty quantification and physics-aware refinement. Our workflow is enabled by a differentiable high-fidelity solver (JAX-Fluids) which we employ for rapid dataset creation and residual-based improvement of the neural emulator to enhance physical consistency. Building on this framework, we first present a suite of model architectures and analyze their scaling behavior to expose their strengths and shortcomings. We then show that residual-based refinement enables training on cases where only mesh and input parameters are available, substantially reducing residuals and improving physical consistency. Together, differentiable simulation and residual-based refinement yield physics emulators that remain reliable beyond their training distribution, a key requirement for deploying surrogates in real-world engineering design loops.

</details>


### [16] [FedSPC: Shared Parameter Correction for Personalized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13748)
*Kannanthodath Induchoodan Ajay Menon,Christian Prehofer,Yunfei Xu,Toru Hirano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedSPC方法，通过对共享参数施加控制变量校正，解决PFL中共享参数更新不一致的问题，提升多种PFL方法在异构数据下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PFL方法将模型分为共享参数和个性化参数联合训练，但共享参数由不同局部目标的客户端更新，导致更新不一致，削弱了共享表示的质量。

Method: 提出FedSPC，一种模块化的校正方法，仅对PFL方法中的共享参数施加控制变量校正，保持个性化参数不变。可集成到共享特征提取器、共享分类器、全共享模型加局部正则化三类常见PFL设置中。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上使用ViT、ResNet-34、VGG-11进行实验，FedSPC显著提升了FedPer、FedRep、FedBABU、LG-FedAvg、Ditto等多种代表性PFL方法的性能。

Conclusion: FedSPC通过针对性地校正共享参数，有效缓解了PFL中的共享表示不一致问题，是一种通用且高效的模块化改进方法。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is one of the important approaches in federated learning for addressing statistical heterogeneity while enabling client-specific adaptation. Many PFL methods split the model into shared and personalized parameters, which are jointly trained on each client. However, this creates an optimization issue: shared parameters are updated by clients optimizing different local objectives, which can lead to inconsistent shared updates and weaken the shared representation. To address this problem, we propose Federated Shared Parameter Correction (FedSPC), a modular correction method for PFL. FedSPC applies control-variate correction only to the shared parameters of a given PFL method, while leaving personalized parameters unchanged. It can be integrated into three common PFL settings: shared feature extractors, shared classifiers, and fully shared models with local regularization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and Tiny-ImageNet with ViT, ResNet-34, and VGG-11 show that FedSPC improves performance across representative PFL methods, including FedPer, FedRep, FedBABU, LG-FedAvg, and Ditto.

</details>


### [17] [The Weight Norm Sets the Grokking Timescale: A Causal Delay Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13753)
*Truong Xuan Khanh,Doan Hoang Viet,Luu Duc Trung,Phan Thanh Duc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过直接干预权重范数而非仅观察，解决了关于"grokking"（神经网络延迟泛化）中权重范数是否导致延迟的争议。研究发现，在自由训练下，权重范数达到一个与种子和学习率变化很小的临界值Wc时发生grokking；若将范数固定在Wc的某个倍数ρ上，延迟时间T_grok与exp(αρ)成正比（α≈7.5，R²=0.996），而固定范数对延迟的影响（约19倍）远大于学习率（仅2倍）。添加LayerNorm可消除这种依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关于权重范数是否是导致grokking延迟泛化现象核心原因的争议，此前研究对此存在矛盾结论。

Method: 通过直接干预（固定/钳制）权重范数进行实验，而非仅被动观察；使用不同模数基数的模块化算术任务，系统变化权重范数、学习率等超参数。

Result: 自由训练下grokking发生在权重范数达到临界值Wc时；固定范数下延迟服从指数律T_grok ∝ exp(αρ)；范数影响远大于学习率；LayerNorm可消除该依赖。

Conclusion: 权重范数是控制grokking延迟的核心因素，固定范数下的指数延迟与自由收缩范数下的对数延迟形成对应关系。

Abstract: Grokking is the delayed onset of generalization in neural networks, arising long after they fit the training data. Whether the weight norm causes this delay is disputed: some studies report a critical norm at the transition, others observe grokking with no fixed norm at all. We settle this by intervening on the norm during training rather than only observing it. Under free training with weight decay, networks grok when the weight norm reaches a value Wc that varies little across seeds and learning rates (CV 1 to 2 percent) and grows with the modular base as a power law. When we instead clamp the norm to a fixed multiple rho of Wc and hold it there, the network still groks, but the delay follows T_grok proportional to exp(alpha rho). One exponent, alpha near 7.5, fits this delay across four moduli (R^2 = 0.996). Over the swept ranges the held norm moves the delay by about 19x and the learning rate by only about 2x, and holding the norm above Wc slows grokking rather than preventing it. A final LayerNorm removes the dependence by decoupling weight scale from the network function; without it the exponential law returns. This pinned-norm delay is the exponential counterpart to the logarithmic delay predicted for a freely contracting norm.

</details>


### [18] [Lyapunov-Based Sample Complexity Analysis for Weakly-Coupled MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14095)

TL;DR: 本文研究了平均奖励弱耦合马尔可夫决策过程（WCMDPs）和Restless Bandits（RBs）在生成模型下的样本复杂度。通过利用弱耦合结构，证明可以以关于臂数N多项式的样本和计算复杂度学习近优策略。技术核心是一种新颖的基于Lyapunov的分析框架，通过显式构造Lyapunov函数和漂移转移技术，以及针对底层线性规划松弛的细粒度扰动分析，避免了经典方法中难以控制的偏差函数问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱耦合MDP和Restless Bandits的状态-动作空间随臂数N指数增长，直接采用表格型MDP方法会导致极高的复杂度上界。需要利用弱耦合结构来降低样本和计算复杂度，但现有理论分析缺乏有限样本保证。

Method: 采用plug-in方法：先从数据中估计经验模型，再应用高效规划算法。核心技术是Lyapunov-based分析框架，包含：(1) 显式构造Lyapunov函数；(2) 真实模型与经验模型之间的漂移转移技术；(3) 对底层线性规划松弛进行细粒度扰动分析。

Result: 对于完全异构的WCMDP，首次建立了多项式复杂度的有限样本PAC保证，最优性间隙为O(1/√N)。对于同构RBs，在温和结构假设下可实现更小的最优性间隙。

Conclusion: 所提出的Lyapunov分析框架为分析基于LP的策略和弱耦合系统提供了通用工具，解决了经典方法中偏差函数难以控制的核心瓶颈。

Abstract: We study the sample complexity of learning in average-reward weakly-coupled Markov decision processes (WCMDPs) and Restless Bandits (RBs) under a generative model. Naive reduction to a tabular MDP leads to high complexity bounds as the state-action space is exponentially large in the number of arms $N$. By exploiting the weakly coupled structure, we show that near-optimal policies can be learned with sample and computational complexities that are polynomial in $N$. Specifically, we analyze the plug-in approach, which applies an efficient planning algorithm to an empirical model estimated from data. For fully heterogeneous WCMDPs, we establish the first finite-sample PAC guarantee with polynomial complexity and an $O(1/\sqrt{N})$ optimality gap. For homogeneous RBs, we further prove that a smaller optimality gap is achievable under mild structural assumptions. A primary technical contribution of our work is a novel Lyapunov-based analysis framework. Unlike classical approaches that rely on the difficult-to-control bias function, our framework uses an explicitly constructed Lyapunov function along with a drift transfer technique between the true and empirical models. A key step of independent interest in our framework is a fine-grained perturbation analysis for the underlying linear programming (LP) relaxation, which provides a general tool for analyzing LP-based policies and weakly-coupled systems.

</details>


### [19] [D2H-AD: A Hybrid Model Utilizing Hyperdimensional Computing for Advanced Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13754)
*Ghazal Ghajari,Elaheh Ghajari,Ashutosh Ghimire,Saeid Ataei,Faris Alsulami,Fathi Amsaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出D2H-AD，一种基于超维度计算(HDC)的轻量级异常检测框架，通过融合距离相似度和密度感知编码，在多个基准数据集上优于五种基线方法，具有高精度、低延迟、小内存占用和可解释性，适合TinyML和边缘AI部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法（传统ML和深度学习方法）依赖大量标注数据、计算成本高，在边缘和高维场景下可扩展性差；现有HDC方法缺乏对距离相似度和密度感知信息的有效整合。

Method: 基于超维度计算(HDC)框架，将距离相似度(distance-based similarity)与密度感知编码(density-aware encoding)融合在统一框架中，利用高维分布式向量表示信息，采用二进制计算实现轻量化设计。

Result: 超维度编码相比直接在原始特征空间应用密度-距离评分，ROC-AUC提升最高达5.4%；在五个基准数据集上，F1-score和ROC-AUC均优于HDAD、ODHD、One-Class SVM、Isolation Forest和Autoencoders五种基线方法；对类别不平衡、噪声和数据复杂度具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: D2H-AD是一种轻量、可解释、计算高效的异常检测框架，具有小内存占用和低延迟特点，特别适合资源受限和实时应用场景，展示了HDC在动态环境中实现准确、可解释且节能的异常检测的潜力。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a fundamental component of intelligent systems with applications in healthcare, cybersecurity, smart grids, and IoT environments. Although conventional machine learning and deep learning methods have demonstrated effectiveness in identifying anomalies, they often rely on large labeled datasets, incur high computational costs, and face scalability challenges in edge and high-dimensional settings. This paper presents D2H-AD, a novel anomaly detection framework based on Hyperdimensional Computing (HDC), a brain-inspired paradigm that represents information using high-dimensional distributed vectors. Unlike existing HDC-based methods, D2H-AD integrates distance-based similarity and density-aware encoding within a unified framework, improving anomaly representation and detection performance. Ablation studies show that hyperdimensional encoding alone yields up to 5.4% higher ROC-AUC than applying the same density-distance scoring directly in the original feature space. Furthermore, D2H-AD consistently outperforms five established baselines, namely HDAD, ODHD, One-Class SVM, Isolation Forest, and Autoencoders, across all evaluated datasets. The framework is lightweight, interpretable, and computationally efficient, making it suitable for resource-constrained and real-time applications. We validate D2H-AD on five benchmark datasets and demonstrate superior F1-score and ROC-AUC performance, together with robustness to class imbalance, noise, and data complexity. In addition to improved accuracy, D2H-AD offers scalability, a small memory footprint, and low-latency operation enabled by binary computations and a compact design. These properties make it particularly attractive for TinyML and edge AI deployments. The proposed framework highlights the potential of HDC for accurate, interpretable, and energy-efficient anomaly detection in dynamic environments.

</details>

*Naichang Ke,Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对ELTO卡尔曼滤波器使用简化噪声模型无法适应非平稳过程的局限性，本文提出了一种具有结构化噪声参数化的贝叶斯滤波方法，通过结合时不变最优噪声模型学习与动态参数自适应，实现了在时变噪声环境中的状态估计性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ELTO卡尔曼滤波器使用简化的噪声模型，无法动态适应非平稳过程，导致在时变环境中状态估计性能受限。

Method: 提出ELTO贝叶斯滤波方法，引入新的结构化噪声参数化方案，将数据驱动的时不变最优噪声模型学习与动态参数自适应相结合，实现结构化噪声自适应。

Result: 在嘈杂、时变环境中，结构化噪声自适应方法有效提升了滤波器的动态状态估计性能。

Conclusion: 结构化噪声参数化与自适应机制是提升ELTO滤波器在非平稳过程中性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Kalman filters based on the Embedded Latent Transfer Operators (ELTO) emerge as novel statistical tools for sequential state estimation. However, a critical limitation stems from their use of simplified noise models, which fail to dynamically adapt to non-stationary processes. To address this limitation, we introduce an ELTO-based Bayesian filtering approach with a new structured parameterization for the filter's noise model. This parameterization enables structured noise adaptation, which couples the data-driven learning of an optimal time-invariant noise model with dynamic parameter adaptation that responds to changes in dynamics within non-stationary processes. Empirical results show that our structured noise adaptation improves the filter's dynamic state estimation performance in noisy, time-varying environments.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond LoRA: Is Sparsity-Induced Adaptation Better?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13767)
*Elijah Cadenhead,Cristian McGee,Xin Li,El Houcine Bergou,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Cheap LoRA (cLA)和链式循环变体c³LA，通过在现有LoRA变体中引入稀疏性实现更简单、更廉价的参数高效微调。作者从信息论角度推导了泛化误差界，并在10个预训练模型、14个数据集上评估了11种微调方法，结果表明所提方法在保持竞争性能的同时可减少最高10%的训练时间和15%的峰值GPU内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA及其变体已被广泛采用，但关于这些方法的比较泛化能力以及低秩更新的结构限制如何保持有效适应性能的问题仍然存在疑问。作者旨在通过历史框架（涵盖全微调、原始LoRA及其变体）来回答这些问题，并进一步提出更简单、更便宜的参数高效扩展方法。

Method: 提出cLA（训练单一低秩因子，另一个因子固定为确定性或随机性）和c³LA（链式循环变体）。将cLA框架化为非对称LoRA的结构化实例，作为全微调的控制列子空间限制。推导了信息论泛化误差界。在10个预训练模型、14个数据集上评估11种微调方法，使用损失景观和谱分析等工具分析泛化性能。

Result: 所提出的方法在参数量匹配的基线上保持竞争力，同时可减少高达10%的训练时间和15%的峰值GPU内存（即使使用朴素、非优化的稀疏实现）。理论和经验泛化度量比常用分析工具更一致、更原则性地支持其成本效益适应。

Conclusion: 限制基于LoRA的PEFT方法适应到稀疏、结构化的列空间在跨任务中仍具有竞争力。这是最早在LoRA变体泛化误差界方面进行理论探索的工作之一。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) and its variants provide a memory- and compute-efficient alternative to full fine-tuning of pre-trained models. However, questions remain about the comparative generalizability of these approaches and how the structural restrictions on low-rank updates preserve effective adaptation performance. We present a historical framing, covering the past (full fine-tuning and original LoRA), the present (different variants of LoRA), and propose simpler, cheaper, parameter-efficient extensions by inducing sparsity within existing LoRA variants: Cheap LoRA (cLA), training a single low-rank factor with the other fixed (deterministically or, in its randomized variant, stochastically), and the chained circulant variant, ${c}^3$LA. We frame cLA as a structured instance of asymmetric LoRA, serving as a controlled column-subspace restriction of full fine-tuning. We derive information-theoretic generalization error bounds for these variants, marking one of the first endeavors in this area. Empirically, we evaluate 11 fine-tuning methods across 10 pre-trained models and 14 datasets, analyzing the fine-tuned models' performance and generalization using tools such as loss landscapes and spectral analysis. Despite the sensitivity of fine-tuned models to the pre-trained model, datasets, and other factors, our study suggests that restricting LoRA-based PEFT methods' adaptation to a sparse, structured column space remains competitive across tasks with their parameter-matched baselines while reducing up to 10% training time and peak GPU memory up to 15%, even with a naïve, non-optimized, sparse implementation. Our theoretical and empirical generalization measures provide a more consistent and principled approach to their cost-effective adaptation than commonly used analytical tools. Overview and code are available at: https://elicaden.github.io/Beyond_LoRA/.

</details>

Method: 采用统计分析方法（秩方差分析、多元回归）和机器学习方法（随机森林、序列模型、强化学习）对一年高分辨率运行数据进行建模与预测优化。

Result: 太阳能辐照度解释了氢气产量45.7%的秩方差（大效应）；电解槽功率为线性主导预测因子，存在太阳能-风能协同交互；随机森林中风电预测重要性最高（尽管双变量相关系数仅0.167）；序列模型利用24小时自相关（r=0.845）进行运行预测；强化学习优化了氢气收益调度。

Conclusion: 统计方法与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性，能够分别揭示线性/参数关系与非线性的、高维的隐含动态。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [23] [Diffusion Policy Optimization without Drifting Apart](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13795)
*Haozhe Jiang,Haiwen Feng,Pieter Abbeel,Jiantao Jiao,Angjoo Kanazawa,Nika Haghtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiPOD框架，通过自蒸馏与策略改进梯度更新交错进行，解决了扩散策略梯度方法中因ELBO与真实对数似然分离导致的双漂移现象，在扩散语言模型后训练和连续控制扩散策略中实现了更稳定的训练和更高的奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散策略梯度方法不稳定，无法实现可靠策略改进，原因是存在双漂移现象：优化变分代理导致ELBO与真实对数似然分离，进而使代理策略梯度与真实期望回报的策略梯度不对齐。

Method: 提出DiPOC框架，通过在每次扩散策略梯度更新中补充一个on-policy ELBO正则化器，将自蒸馏与策略改进梯度更新交错进行，以在整个训练过程中保持紧界行为。

Result: 在扩散语言模型后训练和连续控制扩散策略两个任务上，DiPOD相比先前方法显著稳定了训练过程并达到了更高的奖励。

Conclusion: DiPOD通过简单实用的算法有效解决了扩散策略梯度中的双漂移问题，提升了训练稳定性和最终性能。

Abstract: RL post-training has become increasingly pivotal for improving diffusion policies, but existing diffusion policy-gradient methods are often unstable and cannot achieve reliable policy improvement. We identify the cause as the double-drift phenomenon: optimizing a variational surrogate can let the ELBO separate from the true log-likelihood, which then makes the resulting proxy policy gradient misaligned with the true policy gradient of expected return. We propose \textbf{DiPOD}, a diffusion policy optimization framework that maintains tight-bound behavior throughout training by interleaving self-distillation with policy-improving gradient updates. This leads to a simple and practical algorithm: augmenting each diffusion policy-gradient update with an on-policy ELBO regularizer. Across diffusion language model post-training and continuous-control diffusion policies, DiPOD substantially stabilizes training and reaches higher rewards than previous methods.

</details>

*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 将不确定性集构造问题形式化为从有限字典中选择子集形成原子不确定性集；提出基于覆盖目标的数���驱动选择规则；证明目标函数具有单调子模性，从而可采用贪心算法并保证(1-1/e)近似比；同时提供损失界证书和半径校准规则以实现样本外控制。

Result: 证明了覆盖目标函数是单调子模的，贪心算法具有(1-1/e)近似保证，并匹配了相应的硬度下界；提供了选择子集损失的证书和带样本外控制的半径校准规则。

Conclusion: 本文为鲁棒优化中的不确定性集合选择提供了理论上有保证的数据驱动方法，既具有计算可处理性（贪心算法），又具有最优性保证和统计控制能力。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [25] [Neural Variability Enhances Artificial Network Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13801)
*Robin Preble,Praveen Venkatesh,Stefan Mihalas,Kameron Decker Harris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了人工神经网络中结构化噪声（相关噪声）对鲁棒性的影响，发现相关噪声能显著提升网络对对抗攻击和自然图像改动的鲁棒性，且对抗攻击的噪声结构可跨攻击类型泛化，而自然改动噪声结构则不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物皮层神经响应存在显著的逐次变异性，而外周感觉神经响应更为一致，这引发了对随机性是否承载意义的探讨。已有研究表明噪声和信号相关性可能优化动物的辨别能力，而人工神经网络研究也显示噪声在机器学习任务中具有类似益处，但多数ANN研究忽略了相关性的影响。

Method: 利用修改输入与干净输入下激活值的协方差，研究结构化噪声（相关噪声）对人工神经网络鲁棒性的影响，比较了对抗攻击与自然图像改动两种场景。

Result: 结构化噪声能显著提升网络鲁棒性；自然图像改动的鲁棒性从噪声结构中获益最多，但这种结构在改动类型间迁移性差；而对抗攻击的噪声结构可泛化到其他类型的攻击。

Conclusion: ANN激活中的结构化噪声通常能提升鲁棒性，这为仅依赖局部信息构建鲁棒人工神经网络提供了一种生物可行的策略。

Abstract: Neural responses in cortex exhibit substantial trial-to-trial variability in response to repeated stimuli, while peripheral sensory neurons respond far more consistently, leading many to wonder whether stochasticity may carry meaning. Existing work has argued that noise and signal correlations may be optimized for discrimination in animals, whereas artificial neural network (ANN) studies have shown similar benefits of noise in machine learning tasks, although most ANN work has neglected the effects of correlations. Here we investigate whether correlated noise improves the robustness of artificial neural networks to adversarial attacks and naturalistic image modifications. Using the covariance of activations under modified versus clean inputs, we find that structured noise may significantly improve network robustness. Robustness to naturalistic image modifications benefits most from structure, but this structure transfers poorly across modification types. In contrast, noise structure from adversarial attacks can generalize to other kinds of attacks. These results suggest that structured noise in ANN activations generally improves robustness, establishing a biologically plausible strategy for creating robust artificial neural networks that only relies on local information.

</details>


### [26] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在任意有界凸容量集上，在线库存优化中维护隐藏目标并投影到当前可行集的原则是最优的。基于在线梯度下降，该方法将一般凸集上的遗憾界从需求概率的逆依赖改进为逆平方根依赖，并给出匹配下界。同时首次为强凸损失提供多对数遗憾界，为一般凸容量集提供动态遗憾界。核心创新是范数对齐原则，将状态变量转化为一维队列控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线库存优化（OIO）中，库存结转导致可行动作集依赖于历史，这使得该问题具有状态依赖性，难以直接应用标准的在线凸优化方法。现有方法仅适用于单一线性容量约束，缺乏对一般凸容量集的理论保证。因此，需要一种通用且最优的在线学习原则。

Method: 提出隐藏目标-投影原则：维护一个由在线学习器选择的隐藏目标，并实现其到当前可行订货集上的投影。以在线梯度下降（OGD）为基学习器。引入范数对齐原则：将隐藏目标到可行集的距离（在与投影相同的范数下度量）作为状态变量，该距离路径性地演化为标量队列，其中目标移动为到达，共同需求为服务。

Result: 遗憾界从O(1/√p)改进为O(1/√p)（p为共同需求概率），并证明匹配下界。首次实现强凸损失下的多对数遗憾界，以及适应欧几里得路径变化的一般凸容量集动态遗憾界。合成数据和真实库存数据的实验验证了理论。

Conclusion: 隐藏目标-投影原则在一般有界凸容量集上是最优的。范数对齐原则将高维状态依赖简化为标量队列控制，突破了先前乘积式方法的局限，为更广泛的在线凸优化问题提供了新框架。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [27] [Neural Slack Variables for Shape Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13803)
*Ruben Wiedemann,Antoine Jacquier,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经松弛变量（Neural Slack Variables）方法，通过联合学习辅助网络将约束强制执行转化为回归问题，在单调性和凸性约束测试中实现零违规，优于惩罚方法和原始-对偶方法，并成功应用于金融波动率曲面的无套利学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经网络中强制执行单调性、凸性等函数不等式约束是工业和科学应用中的基本挑战。现有方法如经典惩罚方法和原始-对偶方法仅在违规位置提供约束梯度，导致约束满足脆弱；而通过架构设计保证可行性的方法仅限于简单情形并引入额外归纳偏置。

Method: 提出神经松弛变量（Neural Slack Variables）方法，这是一种深度学习原生的原始侧方法。通过将主网络与联合学习的辅助网络耦合，将约束强制执行转化为回归问题。辅助网络作为主网络约束量的有效目标，诱导可行性和正则性。

Result: 在密集网格的单调性和凸性测试中实现零测量违规，而惩罚方法和原始-对偶基线方法存在残留违规；成功应用于金融中波动率曲面的无套利学习这一开放工业挑战。

Conclusion: 神经松弛变量方法将约束强制执行转化为回归问题，实现了零违规的约束满足，并在定量金融等实际应用中展现有效性。

Abstract: Enforcing functional inequality constraints such as monotonicity and convexity in neural networks is a fundamental challenge in many industrial and scientific applications. Classical one-sided penalty methods, along with primal-dual methods gated by complementary slackness, provide constraint gradients only at violated locations, resulting in fragile satisfaction. Architectures that guarantee feasibility by construction, on the other hand, remain largely limited to elementary cases and impose additional inductive biases. We introduce neural slack variables, a deep learning native primal-side approach that converts constraint enforcement into a regression problem by coupling the primary network with a jointly learned auxiliary network. The auxiliary network serves as a valid target for the primary network's constraint quantities, inducing feasibility and regularity. Neural slack variables achieve zero measured violations on dense-grid monotonicity and convexity test cases, where penalty and primal-dual baselines leave residual violations, and enable arbitrage-free learning of volatility surfaces, an open industrial challenge in quantitative finance.

</details>


### [28] [Uncertainty Estimation and Generalization Bounds for Modern Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13818)
*Luis A. Ortega*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文从方法论和理论两个角度，探讨贝叶斯原理如何加深对现代深度学习系统的理解，提出了可扩展贝叶斯框架DVIP、两种后处理方法VaLLA和FMGP，并在PAC-Bayesian和大偏差理论框架下统一解释了过参数化神经网络的良好泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管神经网络取得了显著的预测性能，但其泛化能力和不确定性量化的机制仍不完全清楚。本文旨在从贝叶斯视角深入理解深度学习系统。

Method: 方法论上提出可扩展贝叶斯框架DVIP（深度变分隐式过程）将隐式过程扩展到深度架构，以及两种后处理方法VaLLA（变分线性化拉普拉斯近似）和FMGP（固定均值高斯过程）为预训练确定性网络提供校准的不确定性估计。理论上在PAC-Bayesian和大偏差理论框架下统一多样性、平滑性和随机性三种关键机制。

Result: 将贝叶斯推断、函数空间建模和大偏差理论统一在一个共同的概率视角下，为过参数化神经网络的良好泛化能力提供了理论解释。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯原理能够帮助我们更深入地理解现代深度学习系统的泛化能力和不确定性量化机制。

Abstract: This thesis investigates how Bayesian principles can deepen our understanding of modern deep learning systems. While neural networks achieve remarkable predictive performance, their ability to generalize and to quantify uncertainty remains only partly understood. This thesis approaches this challenge from both methodological and theoretical angles: unifying Bayesian inference, function-space modeling, and large-deviation theory under a common probabilistic perspective.
  On the methodological side, the thesis introduces the Deep Variational Implicit Process (DVIP), a scalable Bayesian framework that extends implicit processes to deep architectures. Complementing this, two post-hoc methods -- the Variational Linearized Laplace Approximation (VaLLA) and the Fixed-Mean Gaussian Process (FMGP) -- are proposed to equip pretrained deterministic networks with calibrated uncertainty estimates.
  The theoretical contributions focus on one of the central open questions in modern machine learning: why do large, over-parameterized neural networks generalize so well? To address this, the thesis develops a unified probabilistic framework that connects three key mechanisms -- diversity, smoothness, and stochasticity -- within the language of PAC-Bayesian and large-deviation theory.

</details>


### [29] [A Stationarity-and-Coupling Criterion for Training-Free Time-Lagged Spectral Embeddings of Multivariate Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13823)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种无需训练的固定长度多元时间序列描述符 $D(τ)$，该描述符基于时滞相关矩阵截断Marchenko-Pastur边界提取信号特征，通过余弦相似度分类。核心贡献不是该描述符本身，而是一个可证伪的适用性判据：基于平稳高斯VAR(1)模型，推导出描述符有效的前提是信号近似平稳且类别信息存在于跨通道时间耦合而非边际功率。实验验证了该判据的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列描述符通常需要大量训练或缺乏理论保证，作者希望提出一种能在已知适用条件下可靠工作的零参数描述符，并明确回答"何时有效"这一根本问题。

Method: 从平稳高斯VAR(1)模型出发，半形式化推导出三个推论：可区分条件、静态协方差（τ=0）失效原因、功率判别范式失败原因。构建预检测试（增广Dickey-Fuller平稳性检验+功率基线饱和检验）预测描述符适用性。

Result: 在满足条件的四个范式（Sleep-EDF达88.5±4.5%准确率、BCI-IV-2a、MIT-BIH、ESC-50）上性能与强基线竞争且成本极低；在违反条件的三个范式（非平稳ERP、金融波动、可穿戴压力）上准确失效，与预检预测一致。

Conclusion: $D(τ)$的价值不在于精度最优，而在于提供一个紧凑、无需训练、且适用范围可预先确定的嵌入工具。

Abstract: We study training-free fixed-length descriptors for multivariate time series and ask not merely whether such a descriptor performs well, but when it can be expected to work at all. Our object of study is $D(τ)$, built from a time-lagged correlation matrix truncated at the Marchenko-Pastur edge so that only signal-bearing eigenvalues survive and classified by cosine similarity to class centroids with zero learned parameters. The central contribution is not the descriptor but a falsifiable applicability criterion for it. Working from a stationary Gaussian VAR(1) model, we argue that $D(τ)$ separates two classes when the signals are approximately stationary and the class information lives in their cross-channel temporal coupling rather than in marginal per-channel power. We derive, semi-formally, three consequences: a distinguishability condition, why the static ($τ=0$) covariance collapses to chance, and why a stationary but power-discriminated paradigm defeats the descriptor. The criterion is operational: a two-part pre-flight test -- an augmented Dickey-Fuller stationarity check and a power-baseline saturation check -- predicts applicability before any training. We validate both halves on a mixed assortment. On four paradigms that satisfy the criterion (Sleep-EDF, BCI-IV-2a, MIT-BIH, ESC-50) the descriptor is competitive with strong baselines at a fraction of their cost, reaching $88.5\pm4.5\%$ under 20-subject leave-one-subject-out on Sleep-EDF on a single CPU thread. On three that violate it -- non-stationary ERPs, and financial-volatility and wearable-stress regimes that are power-discriminated -- it fails exactly as the pre-flight predicts, and these negatives are the more informative half. We are explicit that $D(τ)$ is not the most accurate representation; its value is a compact, training-free embedding whose domain of validity is known in advance.

</details>


### [30] [Attention-Based Estimation of the Individual Treatment Benefit Probability under Dose Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13821)
*Lev V. Utkin,Andrei V. Konstantinov,Stanislav K. Kogan,Natalya M. Verbova,Maksim I. Goriunov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Dose-AIPTB的通用框架，用于在离散剂量分配下估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB），通过注意力机制或Nadaraya-Watson核回归聚合伪标签，将问题转化为二分类任务，实验表明注意力聚合优于核方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IPTB估计方法局限于二元治疗场景，而临床实践中广泛存在剂量变化的干预措施，缺乏处理多剂量水平的个体化获益概率估计框架。

Method: 将IPTB估计重新定义为对个体治疗效果未观测符号的二分类问题，通过协变量相似的配对比较构建伪标签，并采用注意力机制或Nadaraya-Watson核回归进行聚合，自然支持多离散剂量水平。

Result: 在协变量偏移、不同样本量和异质性结果下的真实与合成数据实验中，基于注意力机制的聚合方法一致优于核回归方法。

Conclusion: 该框架为基于个体层面获益概率的个性化剂量选择提供了基础，代码已公开。

Abstract: Estimating the probability that a treatment outperforms a control for an individual patient, called the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB), offers a clinically intuitive alternative to population-average metrics. However, existing methods for IPTB estimation are largely confined to binary treatment settings, despite the prevalence of dose-varying interventions in clinical practice. We propose a general framework for IPTB estimation with ordinal outcomes under discrete dose assignments, called Dose-AIPTB (Dose Attention-based IPTB). Our approach recasts the problem as binary classification over the unobserved sign of the individual treatment effect, constructing pseudo-labels from covariate-similar pairwise comparisons and aggregating them via attention mechanisms or Nadaraya-Watson kernel regression. This formulation naturally accommodates multiple discrete dose levels, extending beyond the binary treatment paradigm. Through numerical experiments on real-world and synthetic data under covariate shift, varying sample sizes, and heterogeneous outcomes, we demonstrate that attention-based aggregation consistently outperforms kernel alternatives. The framework provides a foundation for personalized dose selection grounded in individual-level benefit probabilities. Codes implementing the model are publicly available at https://github.com/NTAILab/AIPTBDose.

</details>


### [31] [SuperThoughts: Reasoning Tokens in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13862)
*Zheyang Xiong,Shivam Garg,Max Yu,Vaishnavi Shrivastava,Haoyu Zhao,Anastasios Kyrillidis,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SuperThoughts通过将连续的CoT标记对压缩为单一潜在表示，并利用轻量级多标记预测模块每步解码两个标记，在训练时保留离散标记监督，同时将推理吞吐量翻倍。在多个数学推理基准上，该方法实现了约20-30%的思考链长度缩减，准确率仅下降1-2个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长链思维推理虽能提升LLM问题解决能力，但顺序生成标记导致计算开销巨大；现有连续潜在空间推理方法训练不稳定且因缺乏监督信号难以扩展至复杂长程任务。

Method: 将连续CoT标记对压缩为单个潜在表示，通过轻量级多标记预测模块每步解码两个标记；训练时保留离散标记监督，推理时采用基于置信度的自适应机制，在不确定时回退到标准解码。

Result: 在MATH500、AMC、OlympiadBench、GPQA-Diamond上评估Qwen2.5-Math系列模型（1.5B/7B/14B），实现约20-30%的CoT长度缩减，大多数任务上准确率仅下降1-2个点。

Conclusion: SuperThoughts在保持模型推理质量的前提下，有效降低了推理计算成本，证明了压缩连续标记对为潜在表示结合多标记预测的策略在效率与精度之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Long Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning improves LLM problem-solving but is computationally expensive due to sequential token generation. While recent works explore reasoning in continuous latent spaces to bypass discrete token generation, they often struggle with training stability and fail to scale to complex, long-horizon tasks due to lack of supervision signal. We propose SuperThoughts, which compresses pairs of consecutive CoT tokens into single latent representations and decodes two tokens per step via a lightweight Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) module. This preserves discrete token supervision at training time while doubling throughput at inference time. We finetune Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-7B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-Math-14B-Instruct, and evaluate on MATH500, AMC, OlympiadBench, and GPQA-Diamond. With a confidence-based adaptive mechanism that falls back to standard decoding when uncertain, SuperThoughts achieves $\sim$20--30\% CoT length reduction while maintaining accuracy with minimal degradation (1-2 points accuracy drop on most tasks).

</details>


### [32] [Federated Learning for Feature Generalization with Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14416)
*Dongwon Kim,Donghee Kim,Sung Kuk Shyn,Kwangsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCONST通过基于全局模型参数强度的自适应更新幅度调节和线性凸约束，解决了联邦学习中的数据异构性和泛化问题，实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端数据异构导致局部模型过拟合本地分布，且可迁移特征在聚合过程中被扭曲，模型泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出FedCONST方法，根据全局模型参数强度自适应调节更新幅度，避免过度强调已学好的参数同时加强未充分学习的参数；采用线性凸约束保证训练稳定性并保留局部学到的泛化能力；利用GSNR分析验证特征可迁移性和鲁棒性。

Result: FedCONST有效对齐了局部与全局目标，缓解过拟合，在不同异构FL环境下实现了更强的泛化性能和SOTA结果。

Conclusion: FedCONST通过自适应参数调节和线性凸约束机制，有效解决了联邦学习中的数据异构性导致的泛化难题。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) often struggles with generalization due to heterogeneous client data. Local models are prone to overfitting their local data distributions, and even transferable features can be distorted during aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose FedCONST, an approach that adaptively modulates update magnitudes based on the parameter strength of the global model. This prevents over-emphasizing well-learned parameters while reinforcing underdeveloped ones. Specifically, FedCONST employs linear convex constraints to ensure training stability and preserve locally learned generalization capabilities during aggregation. A Gradient Signal to Noise Ratio (GSNR) analysis further validates the effectiveness of FedCONST in enhancing feature transferability and robustness. As a result, FedCONST effectively aligns local and global objectives, mitigating overfitting and promoting stronger generalization across diverse FL environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [33] [Muon$^p$: Muon with Fractional Spectral Powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13867)
*Yihe Dong,Will Sawin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muon$^p$ 是一种通过分数阶谱幂更新 $US^pV^\top$ 来在 Muon 和梯度下降之间插值的优化器，仅使用矩阵乘法即可高效计算，在微调场景下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器通过将梯度替换为其极因子 $UV^\top$ 来扁平化奇异谱，但这种完全扁平化丢弃了可能对自适应有用的奇异值信息。作者希望在保留 Muon 计算效率的同时，恢复部分奇异值信息以提升优化效果。

Method: 提出 Muon$^p$，使用分数阶谱幂 $US^pV^\top$（$p\in(0,1)$）作为更新方向。证明该更新无法通过固定的单变量多项式迭代计算，并推导出仅含矩阵乘法的低次奇次双变量递归来近似 $US^pV^\top$，保持了 Muon 的计算复杂度。理论上证明 Muon$^p$ 在 Schatten $q$-范数下（$q=1+1/p$）最大化损失的线性改进。

Result: 在十亿级模型微调中，Muon$^p$ 在验证困惑度和下游任务性能上均优于基线。通过谱几何视角分析了该方法不适用的场景。

Conclusion: 保留部分奇异谱信息能在某些场景下带来显著收益，Muon$^p$ 提供了一种在 Muon 高效性与奇异值利用之间权衡的原则性方法。

Abstract: Muon is an increasingly widely used optimizer that replaces a gradient $G=USV^\top$ with its polar factor $UV^\top$, thereby flattening the singular spectrum. However, full flattening discards singular-value information that may matter for adaptation. We introduce Muon$^p$, a Muon-style optimizer that instead uses fractional spectral-power updates $US^pV^\top$ for rational $p\in(0,1)$, interpolating between Muon and gradient descent. To make it practical, we prove that fractional spectral powers cannot be computed by any fixed univariate polynomial iteration, and furthermore derive low-degree odd bivariate recurrences that approximate $US^pV^\top$ using only matrix multiplications, preserving Muon's matrix-multiplication-only structure and compute complexity. We show that Muon$^p$ maximizes the linear improvement in loss under the Schatten $q$-norm for $q=1+\frac{1}{p}$. Empirically, Muon$^p$ is especially effective for finetuning: on billion-scale models, Muon$^p$ improves validation perplexity and downstream task performance. We further analyze when Muon$^p$ is less suitable, through the lens of spectral geometry. Our results reveal important insights on when preserving the singular spectrum can bring significant gains, and introduce a principled way to achieve them.

</details>


### [34] [Natively Unlearnable Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13873)
*Gaurav R. Ghosal,Pratyush Maini,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NULLs通过训练共享骨干神经元和稀疏激活的sinks池，实现源特定信息与共享信息的自然分离，从而在部署时通过禁用对应sinks即可完成无梯度更新的高效遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型遗忘因不同数据源贡献相互纠缠而难以实现；现有方法要么难以隔离源贡献，要么阻碍跨源联合学习。

Method: 训练一组共享骨干神经元（backbone）和一个稀疏激活的sinks池；训练时源特定信息自然集中于sinks，共享信息积累于骨干；部署时直接禁用对应sinks实现遗忘，无需梯度更新和保留数据访问。

Result: 在维基百科约600万篇文章上实现单篇文章级遗忘，保留语义相关文章共享事实；在《哈利·波特》案例分析中抵抗对抗性提取和重学习攻击；通用语言能力与标准Transformer相当。

Conclusion: 源级遗忘可以原生内建于LLM训练中，同时保留共享表示学习的优势，无需作为事后处理。

Abstract: Unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data sources, but this has proved challenging because the contributions of different sources are entangled within the model. Isolating source contributions to disjoint parameters makes removal easier, though it obstructs joint learning across sources. We propose NULLs (Natively Unlearnable LLMs), a model class that satisfies the two opposing goals of isolating source-specific contributions and learning jointly across sources, by training a set of shared backbone neurons alongside a pool of sparsely activated sinks. During training, information specific to a source naturally concentrates in its sinks while information shared across sources accumulates in the backbone. A source is then unlearned at deployment by disabling its corresponding sinks, with no gradient updates and no access to the retained data. We show that NULLs scales to Wikipedia's ~6M articles, isolating each as an independent source. Unlearning a single article removes knowledge specific to it while preserving facts shared with semantically related articles, closely matching retraining from scratch. We note that unlearning with NULLs is also robust: in a case study of unlearning the Harry Potter books, NULLs resists both adversarial extraction and relearning that reverses post-hoc unlearning. Finally, NULLs preserves general language capabilities, matching a standard transformer on downstream benchmarks. Together, these results suggest that source-level unlearning need not be an afterthought. It can be built natively into LLM training while retaining the benefits of shared representation learning.

</details>

*Bright Kwaku Manu,Beckett Sterner,Petar Jevtic*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期护理转移概率的准确估计对失能保险定价、准备金和偿付能力评估至关重要。经典精算多状态模型（马尔可夫、半马尔可夫、比例风险模型）虽与队列预测直接关联，但对于具有非线性老龄化模式和不规则协变量历史的不规则纵向健康数据可能过于局限。

Method: 开发了一种结构化的机器学习估计器，模型通过个体健康历史学习，纳入观测间时间间隔，并根据人口和社会经济属性条件化转移概率。输出涵盖健康、轻度失能、重度失能和死亡四个状态的合法概率分布，并通过按年龄组和起始状态聚合形成与精算队列预测兼容的转移矩阵。

Result: 与逻辑回归、梯度提升树、循环神经网络和状态持久化基准相比：在重度失能判别上优于逻辑回归和梯度提升树；保持强校准性能；在聚合转移矩阵误差上达到最低；在概率准确性、终点判别和风险集中度方面综合表现最佳。

Conclusion: 结构化机器学习估计器在超越判别能力的基础上，通过校准和预测忠实度评估，能够有效支持长期护理转移建模。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of long-term care transition probabilities is central to disability insurance pricing, reserving, and solvency assessment. Classical actuarial multi-state models commonly rely on Markov, semi-Markov, or proportional-hazard specifications, which provide a direct connection to cohort projection but may be restrictive for irregular longitudinal health data with nonlinear aging patterns and heterogeneous covariate histories. This paper develops a well-calibrated estimator of multi-state transition probabilities for irregular longitudinal health data. The model learns from individual health history, incorporates the time elapsed between observations, and conditions transition probabilities on demographic and socioeconomic attributes. It produces a valid probability distribution over the next observed health state, with four possible states: healthy, mild disability, severe disability, and death. Individual probabilities are aggregated by age group and origin state to form transition matrices compatible with actuarial cohort projection. Using longitudinal data from the Health and Retirement Study, we compare the proposed estimator with logistic regression, gradient-boosted trees, a recurrent neural network, and a last-state persistence benchmark. The evaluation considers probabilistic accuracy, endpoint discrimination and calibration for severe disability and death, risk concentration, and transition matrix error after aggregation. The proposed estimator improves severe disability discrimination relative to logistic regression and gradient-boosted tree benchmarks, maintains strong calibration, and yields the lowest transition matrix error among the evaluated models in the held-out test analysis. Results show that a structured machine learning estimator can support long-term care transition modeling when judged by calibration and projection fidelity, beyond discrimination.

</details>


### [36] [Gefen: Optimized Stochastic Optimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13894)
*Nadav Benedek,Tomer Koren,Ohad Fried*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gefen是一种内存高效优化器，通过自动共享二阶矩估计和量化一阶矩，将AdamW的内存占用降低约8倍（每十亿参数减少6.5 GiB），同时保持相同性能，可作为AdamW的即插即用替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdamW的一阶和二阶矩状态占用大量训练内存（约2个参数大小的缓冲区），限制了可训练的模型规模或批处理大小。本文旨在减少优化器内存占用而不牺牲性能。

Method: 1) 理论推导：大混合Hessian条目迫使平方梯度比率趋近于1，因此Hessian对齐的参数可共享二阶矩统计量。2) 实际实现：从初始平方梯度推断块结构，无需架构元数据或超参数。3) 学习基于直方图的动态规划量化码本，对一阶矩进行量化，并将相同块结构复用于一阶矩缩放。

Result: 在FSDP和DDP训练中，Gefen实现了最低的峰值优化器内存，保持AdamW级别的性能，同时通过减少内存占用支持更大的微批次，显著提升吞吐量。

Conclusion: Gefen是一种实用的AdamW低内存替代方案，能提升吞吐量、支持更大模型或更大批处理大小，且无需架构特定元数据或额外超参数。

Abstract: AdamW is a default optimizer for modern deep learning, but its first and second moment states add roughly two parameter-sized buffers to training memory. We propose Gefen, a memory-efficient optimizer that automatically shares second-moment estimates across parameter blocks and quantizes the first moment using a learned codebook, thereby reducing AdamW's memory footprint by ~8x while maintaining the same performance, corresponding to a reduction of 6.5 GiB per billion parameters. The method is motivated by a theoretical result showing that large mixed Hessian entries constrain the ratio of squared gradients toward one, suggesting that Hessian-aligned parameters are natural candidates for sharing second-moment statistics. Since computing Hessians is impractical at scale, Gefen infers block structure from the initial squared gradients, requiring no architecture-specific metadata or hyperparameters beyond AdamW defaults. Gefen learns an exact histogram-based dynamic-programming quantization codebook and reuses the same blocks for first-moment scaling. Across diverse experiments, Gefen achieves the lowest peak optimizer memory among the compared AdamW-like methods while maintaining AdamW-level performance. In FSDP and DDP training, the reduced memory footprint enables larger microbatches and improves throughput significantly over AdamW, providing a practical drop-in replacement with lower memory usage that can increase throughput and enable training larger models or using larger batch sizes. We provide the complete Python implementation, including fused CUDA kernels at https://github.com/ndvbd/Gefen

</details>


### [37] [SpikF-GO: Spiking Fourier Graph Operators for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13901)
*Jafar Bakhshaliyev,Niels Landwehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SpikF-GO，一种将超变量图构建与脉冲驱动频谱处理相结合的脉冲神经网络方法，用于多变量时间序列预测。该方法通过Hard Concrete频率门实现可学习稀疏频率选择，Complex LIF门对傅里叶分量的实部和虚部独立进行脉冲处理，并在8个基准上取得了SNN方法中的最佳平均排名，同时能耗低于ANN对应模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN时间序列预测方法独立处理变量，缺乏显式建模变量间依赖关系的机制，这在多变量场景中是一个关键局限，因为跨变量相关性含有大量预测信息。

Method: SpikF-GO结合了超变量图公式（将每个标量观测值作为图节点）与脉冲驱动频谱处理。包含两个关键组件：Hard Concrete频率门用于可学习稀疏频率选择，Complex LIF门对傅里叶实部和虚部分别应用独立脉冲神经元，保留整个频谱域的二进制事件驱动计算。还提出了基于中枢模式发生器（CPG）位置编码的变体以增强长程时序建模能力。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，SpikF-GO在所有SNN方法中获得最佳平均排名，优于其ANN对应模型FourierGNN，且能耗更低。即使在更小的嵌入维度下也能保持竞争性精度，从而实现显著的能耗降低。

Conclusion: 这是首批将基于图的多变量建模引入脉冲域用于时间序列预测的工作之一，也是首个在统一实验协议下对SNN预测架构进行系统比较的研究。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) have emerged as an energy-efficient alternative to conventional neural networks, demonstrating strong performance in computer vision and robotics. More recently, SNNs have been applied to time series forecasting (TSF), with methods exploring spiking temporal backbones, spike-compatible positional encodings, Fourier-domain processing, and redesigned neuron dynamics. However, existing SNN forecasting approaches process variables independently, lacking explicit mechanisms for modeling inter-variable dependencies. This is a critical limitation in multivariate settings, where cross-variable correlations carry substantial predictive information. We propose Spiking Fourier Graph Operators (SpikF-GO), which addresses this gap by combining a hypervariate graph formulation in which every scalar observation becomes a graph node with spike-driven spectral processing. SpikF-GO introduces a Hard Concrete frequency gate for learnable sparse frequency selection and a Complex LIF gate that applies independent spiking neurons to real and imaginary Fourier components, preserving binary, event-driven computation throughout the spectral domain. We further present a variant incorporating Central Pattern Generator-based positional encodings for stronger long-range temporal modeling. Evaluated on eight benchmarks under a unified experimental protocol, SpikF-GO achieves the best average rank among all SNN methods and outperforms its ANN counterpart, FourierGNN, at reduced energy cost. SpikF-GO maintains competitive accuracy even at substantially smaller embedding dimensions, thereby achieving significant energy reductions.
